    "id": "FLAG-001",
    "concern": "Cloud lifecycle: the proposed Local provider assumes a persistent compose project directory, but `run_cloud_cli()` currently materializes deploy assets in a per-command temporary directory and deletes them after each action.",
    "status": "addressed",

  },
  {
    "id": "FLAG-002",
    "concern": "Runner inputs: `cloud chain` only uploads the chain spec in the plan, but the core chain runner validates referenced milestone idea files and the example spec stores those as separate `/workspace/ideas/*.txt` inputs.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "FLAG-003",

  {
    "id": "issue_hints-1",
    "concern": "Did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: CLI surface: The brief's deliverable is `megaplan cloud bootstrap <idea-file>` as a thin wrapper around `megaplan init --idea-file --auto-start`, but the plan makes `--plan-name` mandatory. That is a new required argument not present in the requested surface, and the current core init path already auto-generates a name when `--name` is omitted (`megaplan/handlers.py:768-777`).",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "issue_hints-2",
    "concern": "Did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: Secret redaction: The plan explicitly limits redaction to capture-then-emit paths and leaves `logs -f` unredacted, yet `megaplan cloud logs` currently defaults to follow mode (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py:132-133`). Because the deliverable says 'Secret redaction in cloud logs output' without carving out the default path, the plan still leaves the main logs workflow exposed.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "correctness-1",
    "concern": "Are the proposed changes technically correct?: Init path: The proposed `--idea-file` change does not account for the current default naming and persisted state fields using `args.idea` directly (`megaplan/handlers.py:769,777`). If `idea` becomes optional and only `--idea-file` is passed, `slugify(args.idea)` and `state['idea'] = args.idea` will be wrong unless the plan also updates those lines to use the resolved idea text.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "correctness-2",
    "concern": "Are the proposed changes technically correct?: Chain bootstrap: The proposed `cloud chain` wrapper uploads only the top-level spec, but `megaplan.chain.validate_paths()` rejects any missing milestone idea file (`megaplan/chain.py:239-247`), and the bundled example spec points at separate `/workspace/ideas/*.txt` files (`megaplan/cloud/templates/chain.yaml.example:11-23`). As written, a remote `megaplan chain start --spec ...` would fail immediately unless the wrapper also stages the referenced idea files or rewrites the spec paths.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "scope",
    "concern": "Search for related code that handles the same concept. Is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: Core/cloud isolation: The plan treats the 'core commands work without cloud code loaded' requirement mainly as a new test, but the broader problem is in the parser bootstrap itself. `build_parser()` unconditionally imports `megaplan.cloud.cli` (`megaplan/cli.py:1003-1007`), so `main(argv=...)` cannot satisfy the proposed isolation test until parser registration is also made lazy or optional.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "all_locations-1",
    "concern": "Does the change touch all locations AND supporting infrastructure?: Local provider lifecycle: The current cloud CLI materializes deploy assets in a fresh `TemporaryDirectory` for each action and deletes it on exit (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py:113-119,205-214`). A compose-based `local` provider that writes `.env`, bind-mounts `./workspace`, and later needs the same `docker-compose.yaml` for `exec`, `logs`, `down`, and `destroy` cannot work reliably from ephemeral directories. The plan needs persistent local deployment state, not just new provider classes and templates.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "all_locations-2",
    "concern": "Does the change touch all locations AND supporting infrastructure?: Provider glue: The current attach/session plumbing is Railway-specific at both the parser and dispatcher levels (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py:57-62,182-188`). Adding `local` and `ssh` providers needs follow-through on that integration surface too; otherwise the CLI still exposes a Railway-only `--session` override and provider selection path that does not map cleanly onto the new backends.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "callers",
    "concern": "Find the callers of the changed function. What arguments do they actually pass? Does the fix handle all of them?: Launcher path callers: both `megaplan.auto._run_megaplan()` and `megaplan.chain._init_plan()` shell out to a bare `megaplan` executable (`megaplan/auto.py:77-103`, `megaplan/chain.py:321-329`). The brief explicitly calls out `./.venv/bin/python -m megaplan` as the verified launcher because the system Python is too old, so extending these flows with `init --auto-start` and more cloud wrappers broadens an existing caller-path ambiguity unless the plan also standardizes how subprocess invocations locate the correct interpreter.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  }
]

Updated [critique_output.json](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/megaplan/.megaplan/plans/sprint-2-of-the-megaplan-20260421-1254/critique_output.json).

Main remaining concerns I recorded:
- `cloud chain <spec>` still assumes local specs use locally-readable paths, but the repo’s own chain docs/examples use remote `/workspace/...` paths.
- Step 4’s lazy cloud-parser sketch does not match the current `build_cloud_parser()` API shape.
- `cloud status --chain` still depends on `cloud.yaml.mode == chain`, so ad hoc `cloud chain <spec>` runs have no stable way to tell status which remote spec to inspect.
- The reigh migration runbook’s `rm -rf ... && echo > .../MIGRATED.md` sequence is self-invalidating.

I also marked the prior fixes I could verify as resolved, including persistent local deploy state, Railway-only `--session` gating, follow-mode log redaction, optional bootstrap naming, and the `sys.executable -m megaplan` launcher change.

You are already running inside the megaplan harness for this step. Do the requested planning/review/execution work directly. Do NOT invoke the `megaplan` CLI, do NOT read or activate the `megaplan` skill, do NOT start nested megaplan plans, and do NOT recurse into another planning harness.         /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/megaplan





        Idea:
Sprint 2 of the megaplan cloud feature — extend core megaplan ergonomics, add local + ssh providers, ship toolchain extensibility in cloud.yaml, retire the old reigh-megaplan-dev folder.

### Context
Sprint 1 shipped in v0.19.0 on 2026-04-21: built-in megaplan cloud subcommand (init/build/deploy/status/attach/logs/exec/resume/down/destroy) with a Railway provider, extracted from the ad-hoc setup at ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/. Spec schema lives in megaplan/cloud/spec.py, templates in megaplan/cloud/templates/, providers in megaplan/cloud/providers/.

Architectural principle established in sprint 1 and now codified: CORE megaplan owns capabilities, CLOUD subcommands are thin transport wrappers. Do not reinvent chain state parsing in cloud. Do not grow a parallel 'cloud chain *' surface. Core gets the command; cloud wraps it for remote execution.

### Sprint 2 deliverables

Core megaplan additions (must work locally without any cloud dependency):
1. megaplan chain start --spec <path> — validate spec, resolve referenced idea files, kick auto-loop per milestone. Replaces the 'init + auto' pair for chain runs.
2. megaplan chain status --spec <path> — read chain_state.json, pretty-print current_milestone, completed, remaining, per-milestone status. Unit tests against synthetic chain states.
3. megaplan init --idea-file <path> with optional --auto-start flag — read idea from a file instead of the positional CLI arg.

Cloud additions (thin wrappers over the new core commands):
4. megaplan cloud chain <spec.yaml> — upload spec, then cloud exec to call megaplan chain start remotely.
5. megaplan cloud bootstrap <idea-file> — upload idea file, then cloud exec to call megaplan init --idea-file --auto-start remotely.
6. megaplan cloud status --chain — fetch remote chain_state.json, invoke the core chain-status formatter on it. Do NOT reinvent the parser.

New providers:
7. Local provider (docker compose) — fastest iteration target, no Railway/cloud dependency. Becomes the CI-friendly smoke-test target.
8. SSH provider (plain docker-over-ssh) — 'any host with docker + ssh access' story.

Toolchain extensibility:
9. toolchains: block in cloud.yaml — declares one or more toolchains (rust, go, java, or custom with user-provided install snippet). Template renderer appends corresponding Dockerfile stages. Without this, the image is Python/Node only.

Hardening + retirement:
10. Secret redaction in cloud logs output (match known secret name patterns, replace with ***REDACTED***).
11. Clear error messages + documentation link when railway or docker CLI is missing at runtime.
12. ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/ migration — deploy cloud with a cloud.yaml matching reigh's current env, run a tiny plan to verify parity, then delete the folder and replace with a MIGRATED.md pointer.

### Hard constraints
- Core commands MUST work locally without any cloud code loaded.
- Cloud wrappers MUST call out to core commands, not reimplement.
- Fly provider is OPTIONAL (skip if time-pressured).
- Preserve v0.19.0's public surface; this is additive.
- No breaking cloud.yaml changes from v0.19.0 (add fields, don't rename).
- Unit tests for every new core command; mocked-subprocess tests for every new provider; smoke test for local provider docker-compose lifecycle.

### Explicit non-goals
- No Kubernetes provider.
- No chain-of-chains.
- No cloud-specific chain status parser (the whole point of the core-first architecture).
- No version bump in this plan — sprint 2 ships as v0.20.0 but the bump + CHANGELOG commit is a separate follow-up.

### References
- Sprint 1 landed in v0.19.0 (2026-04-21). Full CHANGELOG entry in CHANGELOG.md.
- Reigh parity reference: ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/ and megaplan/cloud/_reference/ (gitignored local copy of same).
- Verified launcher: ./.venv/bin/python -m megaplan (system python is 3.8, fails on NotRequired).
- Sprint 1 target retrospective: cloud subcommand CLI is already wired via megaplan/cloud/cli.py and dispatched from megaplan/cli.py.

Robustness: standard — feature-rich with architecture decisions to enforce, multiple independent pieces worth independent critique, gate useful for scope discipline.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Cloud Sprint 2 — Core Ergonomics, Local/SSH Providers, Toolchain Block, Reigh Retirement (Rev 3)

## Overview

**Goal.** Three new **core** commands (`chain start`, `chain status` pretty-print, `init --idea-file`/`--auto-start`) with zero cloud dependency, three **cloud** wrappers that thin-call them (`cloud chain`, `cloud bootstrap`, `cloud status --chain`), two new **providers** (`local` docker-compose, `ssh` docker-over-ssh), a **toolchains** extensibility block in `cloud.yaml`, full log **secret redaction** (including the default `logs -f` path), clear missing-CLI errors, and retirement of `~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/`.

**Architectural contract (from sprint 1).** Core owns capability; cloud wraps transport. No re-parsing `chain_state.json` in cloud code.

**What Rev 3 changes vs Rev 2 (surgical — five spots).** Per gate v2 guidance, Rev 3 keeps Rev 2's structure intact and only rewrites the sections that had concrete mechanism bugs:
- **Step 4 parser mechanism** — split `build_cloud_parser` so the lazy path can register cloud subcommands on a *fresh standalone parser*, not reparse tail args against a mismatched shape. (resolves FLAG-003, FLAG-005, correctness-1)
- **Step 15 `cloud chain` path contract** — explicit local↔remote mapping. Spec paths are treated as remote; local sources come from `--idea-dir` (default = spec's parent), matched by stripping the `spec.repo.workspace` prefix. (resolves FLAG-002, correctness-2)
- **Step 15+16 ad-hoc chain transport** — `cloud chain` persists a `last_chain.json` marker under the persistent deploy dir; `cloud status --chain` reads marker → falls back to `spec.chain.spec` → accepts `--remote-spec` override. (resolves FLAG-006, issue_hints-1, callers)
- **Step 19 runbook order** — write `MIGRATED.md` before clearing the folder contents. (resolves FLAG-007, issue_hints-2)
- **`docs/cloud.md` explicitly in scope** — added as a MUST documentation target since README line 108–113 points users there as the authoritative cloud reference. (resolves scope, all_locations)

Everything else from Rev 2 stays untouched.

**Current shape** (unchanged from Rev 2).
- Core: `megaplan/chain.py` has `ChainSpec`/`ChainState`/`load_chain_state`/`validate_paths`, drives via `auto.drive()`. `megaplan/handlers.py:handle_init` takes `idea` as a positional arg only.
- Cloud: `megaplan/cloud/{cli.py,spec.py,template.py,providers/base.py,providers/railway.py}`. `spec.py:167-172` rejects non-railway providers. `providers/base.py:52-54` has a hardcoded `if name != "railway"` guard. `cli.py` uses `TemporaryDirectory` for deploy dirs. `build_cloud_parser(subparsers)` currently adds a top-level `cloud` entry under the parent subparsers and then registers subcommands on it (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py:22-27`).

**Constraints** (unchanged).
- No core module imports anything from `megaplan.cloud` (verified by both code + test).
- `cloud.yaml` v0.19.0 spec keeps loading unchanged; all additions are additive.
- Fly provider skipped (brief: optional).
- No version bump / CHANGELOG in this plan — separate follow-up commit.

## Phase 1: Core-first commands + parser & launcher hygiene

### Step 1: Add `megaplan chain start` subcommand (`megaplan/chain.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Extend** `build_chain_parser` (`megaplan/chain.py:528`) with a `start` subparser under `chain_sub`. Flags: `--spec <path>` required, `--no-git-refresh`. Keep the bare `megaplan chain --spec` path working for backcompat with existing wrappers.
2. **Wire** `run_chain_cli` (`megaplan/chain.py:558`) to dispatch `chain start` to the same `run_chain(...)` driver the bare command uses.
3. **Update** `megaplan/cloud/wrappers/mp-chain` to call `megaplan chain start --spec "$1"`.

### Step 2: Add `chain status` pretty-print formatter (`megaplan/chain.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `format_chain_status(spec: ChainSpec, state: ChainState) -> dict` in `megaplan/chain.py` returning `current_milestone` (`{label, index}` or `None`), `completed`, `remaining`, `per_milestone` (status ∈ `completed | in_progress | pending`), plus pass-through `seed_plan`/`current_plan_name`/`last_state`.
2. **Rewrite** the `chain status` branch in `run_chain_cli`: emit the structured JSON to stdout (new `summary` key = formatter output; existing `success`/`spec`/`milestone_count`/`seed_plan`/`chain_state` keys preserved) and a human-readable block to stderr via `writer`.
3. **Export** `format_chain_status` so `cloud status --chain` (Step 16) reuses it verbatim.

### Step 3: `megaplan init --idea-file` with optional `--auto-start` (`megaplan/cli.py`, `megaplan/handlers.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Adjust** `init_parser` (`megaplan/cli.py:835-852`): make `idea` positional optional (`nargs="?"`); add `--idea-file <path>` and `--auto-start`.
2. **Update** `handle_init` (`megaplan/handlers.py:728`). At the top, compute `idea_text: str`:
   - Both positional + `--idea-file` → `CliError("invalid_args", "--idea-file is mutually exclusive with the positional idea argument")`.
   - `--idea-file`: read UTF-8, strip; empty → `CliError("invalid_args", "idea file is empty")`.
   - Positional: use `args.idea`.
   - Neither: `CliError("invalid_args", "either positional idea or --idea-file is required")`.
   Replace **every** `args.idea` reference (currently `handlers.py:769` `slugify(args.idea)` and `:777` `"idea": args.idea`) with `idea_text`.
3. **`--auto-start` path:** after `save_state(...)` and the init response is built, call `megaplan.auto.drive(plan_name, cwd=root, ...)` in-process with default driver knobs; embed `DriverOutcome` fields under `auto_outcome` in the response.

### Step 4: Lazy cloud subparser registration with a refactored helper (`megaplan/cli.py`, `megaplan/cloud/cli.py`) — resolves FLAG-003, FLAG-005, correctness-1, scope
**Scope:** Small
1. **Refactor** `megaplan/cloud/cli.py::build_cloud_parser`. Split it into two functions that share the subcommand registration body:
   ```python
   def _register_cloud_subcommands(cloud_parser: argparse.ArgumentParser) -> None:
       # The current body of build_cloud_parser from "cloud_sub = cloud_parser.add_subparsers(...)"
       # downward — all subcommand registrations (init/build/deploy/status/attach/logs/exec/resume/down/destroy
       # plus the new chain/bootstrap from Step 15).
       ...

   def build_cloud_parser(subparsers: Any) -> None:
       cloud_parser = subparsers.add_parser("cloud", help="Manage provider-backed megaplan cloud runners")
       _register_cloud_subcommands(cloud_parser)
   ```
   This preserves the existing under-subparsers API for anyone who uses it (today only `megaplan/cli.py`); the new `_register_cloud_subcommands` can register on any parser object.
2. **Replace** the unconditional import at `megaplan/cli.py:1006-1007` with a stub + deferred dispatch:
   - In `build_parser`, register a stub `cloud` entry that swallows all remaining args: `cloud_stub = subparsers.add_parser("cloud", help="Provider-backed megaplan cloud runners (loaded on demand)", add_help=False); cloud_stub.add_argument("cloud_args", nargs=argparse.REMAINDER)`. The stub parses but does not dispatch.
   - In `main`, **intercept before `parser.parse_known_args`**: if `argv and argv[0] == "cloud"`, lazy-import `from megaplan.cloud.cli import _register_cloud_subcommands, run_cloud_cli`, build a fresh standalone `cloud_parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="megaplan cloud")`, call `_register_cloud_subcommands(cloud_parser)`, then `cloud_args = cloud_parser.parse_args(argv[1:])`. Dispatch via `run_cloud_cli(root, cloud_args)`. This bypasses the top-level parser entirely for the cloud path — no "reparse tail args against a mismatched parent shape."
3. **`megaplan cloud --help` discoverability:** the early-intercept branch fires for `argv[0] == "cloud"` regardless of what follows, so `["cloud", "--help"]` hits the standalone parser and argparse emits the usual subparser help block listing every cloud subcommand. Test in Step 6 asserts this.
4. **Isolation guarantee:** when `argv[0] != "cloud"`, the stub parser is all that exists; no `megaplan.cloud.*` module is imported. This is what Step 6's isolation test verifies.

### Step 5: Standardize subprocess launcher (`megaplan/auto.py`, `megaplan/chain.py`) — resolves callers (already addressed in Rev 2, unchanged)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Replace** `["megaplan", *args]` at `megaplan/auto.py:97` with `[sys.executable, "-m", "megaplan", *args]`.
2. **Replace** `["megaplan", "init", ...]` / `["megaplan", "status", ...]` at `megaplan/chain.py:323, 271` with the same prefix.
3. **Keep** bare `megaplan` in shell wrappers under `megaplan/cloud/wrappers/` — they run inside the container where `megaplan` is on PATH.
4. **Test update:** any `tests/test_auto.py` / `tests/test_chain.py` argv assertions get the new prefix.

### Step 6: Core tests (`tests/test_chain.py`, `tests/test_handle_init_idea_file.py`, `tests/test_core_without_cloud.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Extend** `tests/test_chain.py`: `test_chain_start_invokes_driver`, `test_format_chain_status_pretty` (seed+3 milestones).
2. **Add** `tests/test_handle_init_idea_file.py`: `test_init_reads_idea_file`, `test_init_rejects_both_positional_and_idea_file`, `test_init_rejects_empty_idea_file`, `test_init_slugify_uses_resolved_idea_text`, `test_init_state_idea_is_file_contents`, `test_init_auto_start_invokes_drive`.
3. **Add** `tests/test_core_without_cloud.py`:
   - Uses a `subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-c", ...])` subprocess with `PYTHONPATH` controlled so the child has no `megaplan.cloud` importable path (monkeypatch or a sitecustomize stub that raises on `megaplan.cloud` import). Runs `main(["chain", "status", "--spec", ...])` and `main(["init", "--project-dir", ..., "--idea-file", ..., "--auto-start"])` — both must succeed.
   - Separate `test_cloud_help_still_discoverable`: runs `main(["cloud", "--help"])`, asserts exit 0 and the output mentions every cloud subcommand (existing + new `chain`/`bootstrap`).

## Phase 2: Cloud infrastructure refactors + providers

### Step 7: Persistent per-project deploy-dir state (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py`) — unchanged from Rev 2
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Introduce** `_persistent_deploy_dir(spec: CloudSpec) -> Path`:
   - Railway → `TemporaryDirectory` context (stateless; `railway up` pushes).
   - Local → `~/.megaplan/cloud/<compose_project>/` (mkdir -p), persistent.
   - SSH → `~/.megaplan/cloud/ssh-<host>/`, persistent.
2. **Refactor** `run_cloud_cli` so `_materialized_deploy_dir` is provider-aware. `destroy` is the only command that clears the persistent dir (after down succeeds).
3. **Dispatch change:** local/ssh actions (`build`/`deploy`/`exec`/`logs`/`down`/`destroy`) all work from the same stable dir so `docker-compose.yaml` and `.env` are always findable.
4. **Test** (`tests/test_cloud_persistent_dir.py`): `build → exec → down` on a `provider: local` spec reuses the same materialized dir.

### Step 8: Generalize session/attach glue (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py`) — unchanged from Rev 2
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `Provider.supports_session: bool` class attribute. Railway overrides to `True`; Local/SSH inherit `False`.
2. **Enforce** in `run_cloud_cli`: `getattr(args, "session", None)` + `not provider.supports_session` → `CliError("invalid_args", "--session is only supported for provider: railway")`.
3. **Test** (`tests/test_cloud_cli_session.py`).

### Step 9: Unlock `local` and `ssh` providers in the spec loader (`megaplan/cloud/spec.py`) — unchanged from Rev 2
**Scope:** Small
1. **Accept** `"railway" | "local" | "ssh"` in `load_spec`. `FUTURE_PROVIDERS = ("fly",)` with a distinct error ("Fly provider is deferred to a future sprint; use railway, local, or ssh for now.").
2. **Add** `LocalSpec(compose_project, workdir)` and `SshSpec(host, user, port, identity_file, remote_dir, container)` dataclasses; parse when provider matches.
3. **Add** `local: LocalSpec | None = None` / `ssh: SshSpec | None = None` to `CloudSpec`.
4. **Update tests:** drop reject-sprint-2 test; add happy paths + Fly future-provider error test.

### Step 10: `Provider` base + registry + file-transfer hooks (`megaplan/cloud/providers/base.py`, `providers/railway.py`) — unchanged from Rev 2
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `Provider.upload_file(src, dest)` and `Provider.read_remote_file(path)` hooks — base raises `CliError("not_implemented", ...)`; every provider overrides.
2. **Replace** `providers/base.py:52-54` hardcoded guard with a lazy `{"railway": ..., "local": ..., "ssh": ...}` factory dict.
3. **Implement** on `RailwayProvider`: `railway ssh -- "base64 -d > dest"` (stdin base64) for upload; `railway ssh -- cat <path>` for read.

### Step 11: Local provider (`megaplan/cloud/providers/local.py`, `megaplan/cloud/templates/docker-compose.yaml.tmpl`) — unchanged from Rev 2
**Scope:** Medium
1. Single-container `agent` service in `docker-compose.yaml.tmpl`, bind-mounted `./workspace` inside the persistent deploy dir, env via `.env`, port per `resources.port`.
2. `LocalProvider` implements every ABC method with `docker compose -p <proj> -f <deploy>/docker-compose.yaml ...`. All operate on the persistent deploy dir from Step 7.
3. Guard construction: missing `docker` → `CliError("provider_unavailable", ...)` with install link (Step 18 standardizes).
4. `template.py` materializes `docker-compose.yaml` when `spec.provider == "local"`.

### Step 12: SSH provider (`megaplan/cloud/providers/ssh.py`) — unchanged from Rev 2
**Scope:** Medium
1. `SshProvider` reads `SshSpec`; `_remote_run(cmd)` uses `ssh [-i] [-p] user@host -- bash -lc <shlex.quote(cmd)>`.
2. `build` rsyncs the persistent deploy dir to `remote_dir`, then `docker build` remotely. `scp -r` fallback if rsync missing (warn, not fail).
3. `deploy` writes `.env` remotely, `docker run -d --name <container> --env-file ... -v ... -p ...`.
4. All methods follow Railway's pattern over `_remote_run`; `shlex.quote` every interpolated user value.

### Step 13: Provider tests (`tests/test_cloud_local.py`, `tests/test_cloud_ssh.py`, `tests/test_cloud_local_lifecycle.py`) — unchanged from Rev 2
**Scope:** Medium
1. Mocked-subprocess unit tests — exact argv assertions.
2. `@pytest.mark.slow` smoke test: materialize → build → deploy → `ssh_exec("which megaplan")` → destroy. Under 5 minutes.

### Step 14: Toolchain extensibility (`megaplan/cloud/spec.py`, `megaplan/cloud/template.py`, `megaplan/cloud/templates/Dockerfile`) — unchanged from Rev 2
**Scope:** Medium
1. `toolchains:` accepts string aliases (`rust`/`go`/`java`) or `{name, install}` mappings.
2. `TOOLCHAIN_BLOCK` placeholder in the Dockerfile between Node install and `COPY entrypoint.sh`; `_TOOLCHAIN_RECIPES` dict in `template.py`.
3. Commented example in `cloud.yaml.tmpl`.
4. Tests: rust recipe, custom snippet, unknown alias rejection, and a byte-identical-baseline diff for the no-toolchain path.

## Phase 3: Cloud wrappers + hardening + migration

### Step 15: `cloud chain` + `cloud bootstrap` with explicit path contract and marker persistence (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py`) — resolves FLAG-002, FLAG-006, correctness-2, issue_hints-1, callers
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** subparsers:
   - `chain` — positional `spec` (local path), optional `--idea-dir <local>` (default: spec's parent directory).
   - `bootstrap` — positional `idea_file` (local path), optional `--plan-name` (omit → core slug default), optional `--robustness` (default `standard`).
2. **`cloud chain` explicit path contract (FLAG-002, correctness-2):**
   - Parse the spec locally with `megaplan.chain.load_spec(spec_path)` — **enumeration only**; no reimplementation.
   - **Spec paths are treated as remote paths** (matches `megaplan/cloud/templates/chain.yaml.example` + `docs/cloud.md`). The bundled example uses `/workspace/ideas/m1.txt` style, and that remains canonical.
   - For each milestone, compute the **local source path** by:
     - If the remote path begins with `spec.repo.workspace + "/"`, strip that prefix → relative tail (e.g. `/workspace/ideas/m1.txt` with `workspace: /workspace` → `ideas/m1.txt`).
     - Resolve against `--idea-dir` (or the spec's parent directory): `local_source = <idea_dir> / <tail>`.
     - If the remote path is absolute but outside `spec.repo.workspace`, or relative, resolve against `--idea-dir` directly.
   - If `local_source` does not exist, raise `CliError("missing_idea_file", "milestone 'X' idea not found on disk at <local_source>. Use --idea-dir to point at the directory containing ideas.")`. Mirrors the remote validator's shape but with an actionable hint.
   - `provider.upload_file(local_source, remote_path)` for every idea file. Also `provider.upload_file(spec_path, remote_spec_path)` where `remote_spec_path = f"{spec.repo.workspace}/chain.yaml"`.
   - Then `provider.ssh_exec(f"cd {shlex.quote(spec.repo.workspace)} && megaplan chain start --spec {shlex.quote(remote_spec_path)}")`.
3. **Persist chain marker (FLAG-006, callers):** after a successful `cloud chain` invocation, write `{ "remote_spec": <remote_spec_path>, "started_at": <utc_iso> }` to `<persistent_deploy_dir>/last_chain.json`. This gives `cloud status --chain` a stable caller-visible way to discover the remote spec path for ad-hoc chains without requiring `cloud.yaml.mode == "chain"`.
4. **`cloud bootstrap`:**
   - Upload idea file to `{workspace}/idea.txt`.
   - Build the remote command, passing `--name` only when `args.plan_name` is set:
     ```python
     cmd = f"cd {wx} && megaplan init --project-dir {wx} --idea-file {ideaq} --auto-start --robustness {rq}"
     if args.plan_name:
         cmd += f" --name {shlex.quote(args.plan_name)}"
     ```
   - `provider.ssh_exec(cmd)`; relay output.
5. **Cloud-side isolation invariant preserved:** `chain.load_spec` is used for enumeration only; no chain-state parsing in cloud code.

### Step 16: `cloud status --chain` with marker → fallback → override (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py`) — resolves FLAG-006, issue_hints-1, callers
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `--chain` + `--remote-spec <path>` flags to the existing `cloud status` subparser.
2. **Resolve the remote spec path** in this precedence order:
   1. Explicit `--remote-spec` argument (highest precedence).
   2. `<persistent_deploy_dir>/last_chain.json::remote_spec` if the marker exists.
   3. `spec.chain.spec` from `cloud.yaml` when `spec.mode == "chain"` (original Rev 2 path).
   4. Otherwise, `CliError("missing_remote_spec", "Unable to locate remote chain spec. Pass --remote-spec <path>, run `cloud chain <spec>` first, or set mode: chain in cloud.yaml.")`.
3. **Implementation:** fetch the remote chain state via `provider.read_remote_file(chain._state_path_for(Path(remote_spec)))`. Parse with `ChainState.from_dict(json.loads(...))`. Fetch the remote spec YAML, write to a tempfile locally, parse with `chain.load_spec(...)`. Call `chain.format_chain_status(spec, state)`. Emit structured + pretty payload matching the local `chain status` output.
4. **Contract test** (`tests/test_cloud_chain_status.py`):
   - Mock `provider.read_remote_file`; assert the three resolution branches each produce the right `remote_spec` path.
   - Assert the rendered payload is **structurally identical** to local `chain status --spec <same inputs>` (guardrail for "no cloud-specific chain status parser").

### Step 17: Secret redaction incl. follow-mode streaming (`megaplan/cloud/redact.py`, `megaplan/cloud/providers/*`) — unchanged from Rev 2
**Scope:** Medium
1. `redact(text, secret_names, env=None)` — literal values (len ≥ 8), `NAME=value` / `NAME: value` patterns, and `sk-.../ghp_.../xoxb-...` regexes → `***REDACTED***`.
2. `stream_redact(proc, secret_names, env=None)` — line-buffered Popen-iterator wrapper for stdout + stderr; flushes short-read tails on EOF.
3. Wire at `_relay_output`, `logs(follow=False)` (capture-then-redact), and `logs(follow=True)` (Popen with `stdout=PIPE, stderr=PIPE, bufsize=1, text=True` → `stream_redact`). `attach` stays a documented gap (interactive pty).
4. `_logs_follow(argv)` helper factored across Railway/Local/SSH.
5. Tests: `tests/test_cloud_redact.py` (table-driven) + `tests/test_cloud_logs_follow_redacted.py` (Popen-mocked per provider).

### Step 18: Missing-CLI errors with install links (`megaplan/cloud/providers/{base,railway,local,ssh}.py`) — unchanged from Rev 2
**Scope:** Small
1. `_missing_cli_error(binary, install_url)` helper in `base.py`.
2. Wire in constructors: Railway → `https://docs.railway.app/develop/cli`; Local → `https://docs.docker.com/get-docker/`; SSH → "already bundled with most systems; on Windows install OpenSSH via Optional Features."
3. `tests/test_cloud_provider_unavailable.py` monkey-patches `shutil.which`.

### Step 19: Retire `~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/` + update `docs/cloud.md` + `README` (`docs/cloud-migration-from-reigh.md`, `docs/cloud.md`, `README.md`) — resolves FLAG-007, issue_hints-2, scope, all_locations
**Scope:** Small
1. **Draft** `docs/cloud-migration-from-reigh.md`:
   - `./.venv/bin/python -m megaplan cloud init --cloud-yaml ./cloud.yaml` (note the launcher).
   - Copy reigh `.env` values into local env or a `.gitignore`-covered `.env`.
   - `megaplan cloud build && megaplan cloud deploy`.
   - `megaplan cloud bootstrap ./idea.txt` (no `--plan-name`) for a tiny parity plan; `megaplan cloud status` → expect `done`.
   - **Cleanup (safe order — resolves FLAG-007, issue_hints-2):**
     ```bash
     # Write the pointer FIRST, then clear the folder contents.
     cat > ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/MIGRATED.md <<'EOF'
     This folder has been migrated into the built-in `megaplan cloud` subcommand.
     See docs/cloud-migration-from-reigh.md in the megaplan repo for the runbook.
     EOF
     # Remove every sibling but leave the directory + MIGRATED.md intact.
     find ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev -mindepth 1 ! -name MIGRATED.md -print0 \
       | xargs -0 rm -rf
     ```
   The sequence writes the pointer before any deletions so the file always ends up in place.
2. **Update** `docs/cloud.md` (README line 108–113 points users here as the authoritative cloud reference; resolves scope / all_locations):
   - Add sections for `provider: local` and `provider: ssh` with spec examples.
   - Add `cloud chain <spec>` workflow explaining the explicit local↔remote path contract (Step 15) — including the `--idea-dir` flag and the default-to-spec-parent behavior.
   - Add `cloud bootstrap <idea-file>` showing the no-`--plan-name` form as the default.
   - Add `cloud status --chain` with precedence order (marker > `spec.chain.spec` > `--remote-spec`).
   - Document the `toolchains:` block with at least one alias + one custom example.
   - Note the follow-mode log redaction behavior and the `attach` interactive gap.
3. **Update** `README.md` "Cloud runs" section to briefly mention the new providers + toolchain block and point at `docs/cloud.md` for the full reference (keeps the existing `README.md:108-113` pointer intact).
4. **Update** `megaplan/cloud/templates/cloud.yaml.tmpl` with a commented `toolchains:` example and commented local/ssh provider sections.
5. **Deliverable from this plan** = the two docs (`cloud-migration-from-reigh.md`, updated `cloud.md`), the README touch-up, the template comments, and the `cloud bootstrap` flow. The actual `rm -rf` / `MIGRATED.md` is human-executed; the runbook's new order is automation-safe.

---

## Execution Order
1. **Phase 1 (Steps 1–6).** Core commands + parser isolation + launcher fix. Step 4 (lazy cloud subparser via the new `_register_cloud_subcommands` helper) and Step 5 (`sys.executable -m megaplan`) must land before Step 6's isolation + `cloud --help` tests.
2. **Phase 2 foundations (Steps 7, 8, 9, 10)** before concrete providers (11, 12).
3. **Concrete providers (Steps 11, 12) + tests (13)** can land in parallel.
4. **Toolchain (Step 14)** independent; any time post-Phase 1.
5. **Phase 3 Step 15** before Step 16 (status reads the marker Step 15 writes). Step 17 after providers exist.
6. **Step 19 (docs + reigh retirement)** last.

## Validation Order
1. Core unit tests first: `pytest tests/test_chain.py tests/test_handle_init_idea_file.py tests/test_core_without_cloud.py -x`. Proves core/cloud isolation.
2. Launcher-path regression: `pytest tests/test_auto.py tests/test_chain.py -x`.
3. Cloud unit tests: `pytest tests/test_cloud_spec.py tests/test_cloud_persistent_dir.py tests/test_cloud_cli_session.py tests/test_cloud_local.py tests/test_cloud_ssh.py tests/test_cloud_template.py tests/test_cloud_redact.py tests/test_cloud_logs_follow_redacted.py tests/test_cloud_provider_unavailable.py tests/test_cloud_chain_status.py -x`.
4. Chain wrapper integration: `pytest tests/test_cloud_chain_wrapper.py -x` — asserts `cloud chain <spec>` resolves every milestone's local source via the Step-15 path contract and writes the marker file.
5. Full suite (`pytest`) — no regression; v0.19.0 `cloud.yaml` surface intact.
6. `@pytest.mark.slow` smoke: `pytest -m slow tests/test_cloud_local_lifecycle.py` on a host with docker.
7. Manual end-to-end: `provider: local` + `mode: idle` + `cloud bootstrap ./idea.txt` (no `--plan-name`) → `cloud status --chain` via marker → `cloud destroy`.
8. Reigh migration — human-run per Step 19 runbook.

That does not match the current API shape: `build_cloud_parser()` registers a top-level `cloud` command under an argparse subparser (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py:22-27`), so reparsing only tail args like `['status']` would be invalid unless the helper signature or parse strategy changes as part of the plan. The lazy-parser direction is right, but the concrete mechanism is still technically inconsistent with the repo.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "correctness-2",
    "concern": "Are the proposed changes technically correct?: Runner inputs: Recurring debt: Rev 2 now enumerates and uploads every `milestones[*].idea` path, but it still assumes those paths are usable as local source paths. The bundled example spec and the cloud docs both tell users to author chain inputs with remote-only `/workspace/...` paths and upload them separately (`megaplan/cloud/templates/chain.yaml.example:1-23`, `docs/cloud.md:123-129`). Under the plan's 'exact-path upload' assumption, that repo-native example would fail the new local preflight with `missing_idea_file` unless the wrapper also defines a local-to-remote path mapping or rewrites the spec before upload.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "scope",
    "concern": "Search for related code that handles the same concept. Is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: Documentation scope still looks narrow relative to how users actually discover cloud behavior. The README explicitly sends users to `docs/cloud.md` for the full cloud reference and the current manual-upload workflow (`README.md:108-113`), but Rev 2's doc success criterion only requires updates to `README` and `cloud.yaml.tmpl`. Because `cloud chain`, `cloud bootstrap`, new providers, and changed upload semantics all alter that detailed reference, leaving `docs/cloud.md` out of the explicit plan still risks a repo where the main cloud reference remains sprint-1-only.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "callers",
    "concern": "Find the callers of the changed function. What arguments do they actually pass? Does the fix handle all of them?: Status caller path: the proposed `cloud status --chain` implementation pulls the remote spec path from `spec.chain.spec`, but the current loader only provides that field when `cloud.yaml` is already in `mode: chain` (`megaplan/cloud/spec.py:218-223`). The new `cloud chain <spec>` wrapper, however, is an independent caller that can start a chain from an uploaded local spec without changing `cloud.yaml` mode. That means the follow-on caller `cloud status --chain` still has no reliable argument source for those ad hoc runs unless the plan also stores the uploaded remote spec path or adds an explicit `--spec`/`--remote-spec` input.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "FLAG-005",
    "concern": "CLI parsing: the lazy cloud-parser implementation sketch reparses `argv[1:]` against a builder that currently registers a top-level `cloud` command, so the proposed mechanism is internally inconsistent unless the parser API changes too.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "FLAG-006",
    "concern": "Chain status transport: `cloud status --chain` still relies on `cloud.yaml.mode == 'chain'` / `spec.chain.spec`, so ad hoc `cloud chain <spec>` runs have no stable caller-visible way to tell status which remote spec/state pair to inspect.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "FLAG-007",
    "concern": "Migration runbook: Step 19's `rm -rf ... && echo ... > .../MIGRATED.md` sequence cannot create the pointer file because it deletes the parent directory first.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "all_locations",
    "concern": "Does the change touch all locations AND supporting infrastructure?: Docs/supporting infra: despite the widened cloud surface, the plan does not explicitly update `docs/cloud.md`, which is the file README points users to for the authoritative cloud workflow (`README.md:108-113`). Because that reference currently documents only the sprint-1 Railway/manual-upload model, the supporting documentation touch points still appear incomplete.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  }
]

      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the mobile action-surface plan is technically incorrect for the current component structure. `submissioncarouselcard` hides `footercontent` below the `md` breakpoint (`src/components/submissioncarouselcard.tsx:219`), while `submissionscarouselpage` also hides the score panel on mobile via `asidecontent={<div classname=\"hidden md:block\">...` and passes `hideactions` to `scorepanel` at `src/pages/submissionscarouselpage.tsx:250-263,347`. reusing that pattern for entrypage would leave mobile users with no navigation controls and no scoring controls.",

        Debt guidance:
        - These are known accepted limitations. Do not re-flag them unless the current plan makes them worse, broadens them, or fails to contain them.
        - Prefix every new concern with a subsystem tag followed by a colon, for example `Timeout recovery: retry backoff remains brittle`.
        - When a concern is recurring debt that still needs to be flagged, prefix it with `Recurring debt:` after the subsystem tag, for example `Timeout recovery: Recurring debt: retry backoff remains brittle`.



        Your output template is at: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/megaplan/.megaplan/plans/sprint-2-of-the-megaplan-20260421-1254/critique_output.json
Read this file first — it contains 5 checks, each with a question and guidance.
For each check, investigate the codebase, then add your findings to the `findings` array for that check.

Each finding needs:
- "detail": what you specifically checked and what you found (at least a full sentence)
- "flagged": true if this describes a difference, risk, or tension — even if you think it's justified. false only if purely informational with no possible downside.
- Every check must end with at least one finding. Never leave a `findings` array empty. If you found no issue, add one detailed `flagged: false` finding explaining what you checked and why it appears clear.

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}
Bad: {"detail": "No issue found", "flagged": false}  ← too brief, will be rejected
Bad: {"detail": "The hints suggest approach X but the plan uses Y. However Y is consistent with X's intent.", "flagged": false}  ← a different approach than the hints IS a flag. You found a divergence — flag it. The gate decides if it's acceptable.

After filling in checks, add any additional concerns to the `flags` array (e.g., security, performance, dependencies).
Use the standard format (id, concern, category, severity_hint, evidence). This array can be empty.

Workflow: read the file → investigate → read file again → add finding → write file back. Repeat for each check.

This is critique iteration 3. The template file includes prior findings with their status. Verify addressed flags were actually fixed, re-flag if inadequate, and check for new issues introduced by the revision.

        Additional guidelines:
        - Robustness level: standard. Use balanced judgment. Flag significant risks, but do not spend flags on minor polish or executor-obvious boilerplate.
        - Over-engineering: prefer the simplest approach that fully solves the problem.

        - Verify that the plan follows the expected structure when validator warnings or the outline suggest drift.

You are already running inside the megaplan harness for this step. Do the requested planning/review/execution work directly. Do NOT invoke the `megaplan` CLI, do NOT read or activate the `megaplan` skill, do NOT start nested megaplan plans, and do NOT recurse into another planning harness. You are an independent reviewer. Critique the plan against the actual repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/megaplan





        Idea:
Sprint 2 of the megaplan cloud feature — extend core megaplan ergonomics, add local + ssh providers, ship toolchain extensibility in cloud.yaml, retire the old reigh-megaplan-dev folder.

### Context
Sprint 1 shipped in v0.19.0 on 2026-04-21: built-in megaplan cloud subcommand (init/build/deploy/status/attach/logs/exec/resume/down/destroy) with a Railway provider, extracted from the ad-hoc setup at ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/. Spec schema lives in megaplan/cloud/spec.py, templates in megaplan/cloud/templates/, providers in megaplan/cloud/providers/.

Architectural principle established in sprint 1 and now codified: CORE megaplan owns capabilities, CLOUD subcommands are thin transport wrappers. Do not reinvent chain state parsing in cloud. Do not grow a parallel 'cloud chain *' surface. Core gets the command; cloud wraps it for remote execution.

### Sprint 2 deliverables

Core megaplan additions (must work locally without any cloud dependency):
1. megaplan chain start --spec <path> — validate spec, resolve referenced idea files, kick auto-loop per milestone. Replaces the 'init + auto' pair for chain runs.
2. megaplan chain status --spec <path> — read chain_state.json, pretty-print current_milestone, completed, remaining, per-milestone status. Unit tests against synthetic chain states.
3. megaplan init --idea-file <path> with optional --auto-start flag — read idea from a file instead of the positional CLI arg.

Cloud additions (thin wrappers over the new core commands):
4. megaplan cloud chain <spec.yaml> — upload spec, then cloud exec to call megaplan chain start remotely.
5. megaplan cloud bootstrap <idea-file> — upload idea file, then cloud exec to call megaplan init --idea-file --auto-start remotely.
6. megaplan cloud status --chain — fetch remote chain_state.json, invoke the core chain-status formatter on it. Do NOT reinvent the parser.

New providers:
7. Local provider (docker compose) — fastest iteration target, no Railway/cloud dependency. Becomes the CI-friendly smoke-test target.
8. SSH provider (plain docker-over-ssh) — 'any host with docker + ssh access' story.

Toolchain extensibility:
9. toolchains: block in cloud.yaml — declares one or more toolchains (rust, go, java, or custom with user-provided install snippet). Template renderer appends corresponding Dockerfile stages. Without this, the image is Python/Node only.

Hardening + retirement:
10. Secret redaction in cloud logs output (match known secret name patterns, replace with ***REDACTED***).
11. Clear error messages + documentation link when railway or docker CLI is missing at runtime.
12. ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/ migration — deploy cloud with a cloud.yaml matching reigh's current env, run a tiny plan to verify parity, then delete the folder and replace with a MIGRATED.md pointer.

### Hard constraints
- Core commands MUST work locally without any cloud code loaded.
- Cloud wrappers MUST call out to core commands, not reimplement.
- Fly provider is OPTIONAL (skip if time-pressured).
- Preserve v0.19.0's public surface; this is additive.
- No breaking cloud.yaml changes from v0.19.0 (add fields, don't rename).
- Unit tests for every new core command; mocked-subprocess tests for every new provider; smoke test for local provider docker-compose lifecycle.

### Explicit non-goals
- No Kubernetes provider.
- No chain-of-chains.
- No cloud-specific chain status parser (the whole point of the core-first architecture).
- No version bump in this plan — sprint 2 ships as v0.20.0 but the bump + CHANGELOG commit is a separate follow-up.

### References
- Sprint 1 landed in v0.19.0 (2026-04-21). Full CHANGELOG entry in CHANGELOG.md.
- Reigh parity reference: ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/ and megaplan/cloud/_reference/ (gitignored local copy of same).
- Verified launcher: ./.venv/bin/python -m megaplan (system python is 3.8, fails on NotRequired).
- Sprint 1 target retrospective: cloud subcommand CLI is already wired via megaplan/cloud/cli.py and dispatched from megaplan/cli.py.

Robustness: standard — feature-rich with architecture decisions to enforce, multiple independent pieces worth independent critique, gate useful for scope discipline.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Cloud Sprint 2 — Core Ergonomics, Local/SSH Providers, Toolchain Block, Reigh Retirement (Rev 4)

## Overview

**Goal.** Three new **core** commands (`chain start`, `chain status` pretty-print, `init --idea-file`/`--auto-start`) with zero cloud dependency, three **cloud** wrappers that thin-call them (`cloud chain`, `cloud bootstrap`, `cloud status --chain`), two new **providers** (`local` docker-compose, `ssh` docker-over-ssh), a **toolchains** extensibility block in `cloud.yaml`, full log **secret redaction** (including the default `logs -f` path), clear missing-CLI errors, and retirement of `~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/`.

**Architectural contract (from sprint 1).** Core owns capability; cloud wraps transport. No re-parsing `chain_state.json` in cloud code.

**What Rev 4 changes vs Rev 3 (two mechanism one-liners).** Gate v3 narrowed the remaining gap to two concrete bugs, both residing in Rev 3's text without structural change. Rev 4 fixes only those two spots:

- **Step 4 argv normalization** — `cli_entry()` calls `main(argv=None)` (`megaplan/cli.py:1061-1117`); the lazy intercept keys on `argv[0]`. Add `if argv is None: argv = sys.argv[1:]` as the first line of `main()` so the intercept fires for normal shell invocations. (resolves FLAG-008, correctness)
- **Step 7 + Step 15 marker location** — Rev 3 wrote `last_chain.json` under the "persistent deploy dir," but the persistent dir only exists for local/ssh providers; Railway (the only currently-implemented provider) uses `TemporaryDirectory`. Move the marker to a provider-independent path: `~/.megaplan/cloud/markers/<sha256-of-cloud.yaml-absolute-path>[:16]/last_chain.json`. Step 16 reads from the same derived path. (resolves FLAG-006, callers, issue_hints, issue_hints-1 — all the same underlying issue)

Everything else from Rev 3 stays untouched. Churn is ~5 lines of plan text.

**Current shape** (unchanged).
- Core: `megaplan/chain.py` has `ChainSpec`/`ChainState`/`load_chain_state`/`validate_paths`, drives via `auto.drive()`. `megaplan/handlers.py:handle_init` takes `idea` as a positional arg only.
- Cloud: `megaplan/cloud/{cli.py,spec.py,template.py,providers/base.py,providers/railway.py}`. `spec.py:167-172` rejects non-railway providers. `providers/base.py:52-54` has a hardcoded `if name != "railway"` guard. `cli.py` uses `TemporaryDirectory` for deploy dirs. `build_cloud_parser(subparsers)` adds a top-level `cloud` entry under the parent subparsers (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py:22-27`).
- Entry: `megaplan/cli.py::cli_entry` invokes `sys.exit(main())` with no argv (line ~1061); `main(argv=None)` currently passes `argv` straight to `parser.parse_known_args(argv)` which handles `None` internally via argparse's fallback, but any pre-parse logic (like the new lazy cloud intercept) needs an explicit list.

**Constraints** (unchanged).
- No core module imports anything from `megaplan.cloud`.
- `cloud.yaml` v0.19.0 spec keeps loading unchanged; additions are additive.
- Fly provider skipped.
- No version bump / CHANGELOG in this plan.

---

## Phase 1: Core-first commands + parser & launcher hygiene

### Step 1: Add `megaplan chain start` subcommand (`megaplan/chain.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Extend** `build_chain_parser` (`megaplan/chain.py:528`) with a `start` subparser under `chain_sub`. Flags: `--spec <path>` required, `--no-git-refresh`. Keep the bare `megaplan chain --spec` path working for backcompat.
2. **Wire** `run_chain_cli` (`megaplan/chain.py:558`) to dispatch `chain start` to the same `run_chain(...)` driver.
3. **Update** `megaplan/cloud/wrappers/mp-chain` to call `megaplan chain start --spec "$1"`.

### Step 2: Add `chain status` pretty-print formatter (`megaplan/chain.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `format_chain_status(spec: ChainSpec, state: ChainState) -> dict` in `megaplan/chain.py` returning `current_milestone` (`{label, index}` or `None`), `completed`, `remaining`, `per_milestone` (status ∈ `completed | in_progress | pending`), plus pass-through `seed_plan`/`current_plan_name`/`last_state`.
2. **Rewrite** the `chain status` branch in `run_chain_cli`: structured JSON to stdout (new `summary` key = formatter output; existing keys preserved) + human-readable block to stderr via `writer`.
3. **Export** `format_chain_status` for reuse by Step 16.

### Step 3: `megaplan init --idea-file` with optional `--auto-start` (`megaplan/cli.py`, `megaplan/handlers.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Adjust** `init_parser` (`megaplan/cli.py:835-852`): `idea` → `nargs="?"`; add `--idea-file <path>` and `--auto-start`.
2. **Update** `handle_init` (`megaplan/handlers.py:728`). Compute `idea_text: str` at the top:
   - Both positional + `--idea-file` → `CliError("invalid_args", "--idea-file is mutually exclusive with the positional idea argument")`.
   - `--idea-file`: read UTF-8, strip; empty → `CliError("invalid_args", "idea file is empty")`.
   - Positional: use `args.idea`.
   - Neither: `CliError("invalid_args", ...)`.
   Replace every `args.idea` reference at `handlers.py:769` (slugify) and `:777` (state.idea) with `idea_text`.
3. **`--auto-start` path:** after `save_state(...)` succeeds and the init response is built, call `megaplan.auto.drive(plan_name, cwd=root, ...)` in-process; embed `DriverOutcome` fields as `auto_outcome` in the response.

### Step 4: Lazy cloud subparser registration + argv normalization (`megaplan/cli.py`, `megaplan/cloud/cli.py`) — resolves FLAG-003, FLAG-005, FLAG-008, correctness-1, correctness, scope
**Scope:** Small
1. **Refactor** `megaplan/cloud/cli.py::build_cloud_parser`. Split the existing body:
   ```python
   def _register_cloud_subcommands(cloud_parser: argparse.ArgumentParser) -> None:
       # Existing body from "cloud_sub = cloud_parser.add_subparsers(...)" downward:
       # init/build/deploy/status/attach/logs/exec/resume/down/destroy + new chain/bootstrap (Step 15).
       ...

   def build_cloud_parser(subparsers: Any) -> None:
       cloud_parser = subparsers.add_parser("cloud", help="Manage provider-backed megaplan cloud runners")
       _register_cloud_subcommands(cloud_parser)
   ```
2. **Normalize argv in `main` (FLAG-008, correctness — NEW IN REV 4):** update `megaplan/cli.py::main` to normalize `argv` on entry so the intercept below fires for `cli_entry()` which calls `main()` with no args:
   ```python
   def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
       if argv is None:
           argv = sys.argv[1:]
       # ... rest of the function follows, unchanged below the intercept ...
   ```
3. **Replace** the unconditional cloud import at `megaplan/cli.py:1006-1007` with a stub + deferred dispatch:
   - In `build_parser`, register a stub `cloud` subparser: `cloud_stub = subparsers.add_parser("cloud", help="Provider-backed megaplan cloud runners (loaded on demand)", add_help=False); cloud_stub.add_argument("cloud_args", nargs=argparse.REMAINDER)`.
   - In `main`, immediately after the argv normalization, intercept before any other parsing: if `argv and argv[0] == "cloud"`, lazy-import `from megaplan.cloud.cli import _register_cloud_subcommands, run_cloud_cli`, build a fresh `cloud_parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="megaplan cloud")`, call `_register_cloud_subcommands(cloud_parser)`, then `cloud_args = cloud_parser.parse_args(argv[1:])`, and dispatch via `run_cloud_cli(root, cloud_args)`.
4. **`megaplan cloud --help` discoverability:** the early-intercept branch fires for `argv[0] == "cloud"` regardless of what follows, so `["cloud", "--help"]` hits the standalone parser and argparse emits the normal subparser help block.
5. **Isolation guarantee:** when `argv[0] != "cloud"`, the stub parser is all that exists; no `megaplan.cloud.*` module is imported.

### Step 5: Standardize subprocess launcher (`megaplan/auto.py`, `megaplan/chain.py`) — resolves callers (unchanged)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Replace** `["megaplan", *args]` at `megaplan/auto.py:97` with `[sys.executable, "-m", "megaplan", *args]`.
2. **Replace** `["megaplan", "init", ...]` / `["megaplan", "status", ...]` at `megaplan/chain.py:323, 271` with the same prefix.
3. **Keep** bare `megaplan` in `megaplan/cloud/wrappers/` — those run in-container.
4. **Test updates:** adjust argv assertions in `tests/test_auto.py` / `tests/test_chain.py`.

### Step 6: Core tests (`tests/test_chain.py`, `tests/test_handle_init_idea_file.py`, `tests/test_core_without_cloud.py`, `tests/test_cli_entry.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Extend** `tests/test_chain.py`: `test_chain_start_invokes_driver`, `test_format_chain_status_pretty`.
2. **Add** `tests/test_handle_init_idea_file.py`: `test_init_reads_idea_file`, `test_init_rejects_both_positional_and_idea_file`, `test_init_rejects_empty_idea_file`, `test_init_slugify_uses_resolved_idea_text`, `test_init_state_idea_is_file_contents`, `test_init_auto_start_invokes_drive`.
3. **Add** `tests/test_core_without_cloud.py`: `subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-c", ...])` with `megaplan.cloud` importably absent, runs `main(["chain", "status", ...])` and `main(["init", "--idea-file", ..., "--auto-start"])` — both succeed. Also includes `test_cloud_help_still_discoverable` for `main(["cloud", "--help"])`.
4. **Add** `tests/test_cli_entry.py` (NEW IN REV 4 for the argv-normalization fix): monkeypatches `sys.argv = ["megaplan", "cloud", "status", "--help"]`, calls `cli_entry()` via a thin wrapper that catches `SystemExit`, asserts the lazy cloud intercept fires and help prints. Guardrails the FLAG-008 regression permanently.

## Phase 2: Cloud infrastructure refactors + providers

### Step 7: Persistent per-project deploy-dir state + provider-independent marker location (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py`) — resolves FLAG-001, FLAG-006, all_locations-1, callers, issue_hints
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Introduce** `_persistent_deploy_dir(spec: CloudSpec) -> Path`:
   - Railway → `TemporaryDirectory` context (stateless; `railway up` pushes).
   - Local → `~/.megaplan/cloud/<compose_project>/` (mkdir -p), persistent.
   - SSH → `~/.megaplan/cloud/ssh-<host>/`, persistent.
2. **Introduce (NEW IN REV 4)** `_marker_dir(cloud_yaml_path: Path) -> Path`. This is provider-independent and lives at a stable location regardless of whether the deploy dir is ephemeral (Railway) or persistent (Local/SSH):
   ```python
   def _marker_dir(cloud_yaml_path: Path) -> Path:
       sha = hashlib.sha256(str(cloud_yaml_path.resolve()).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
       path = Path.home() / ".megaplan" / "cloud" / "markers" / sha
       path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
       return path
   ```
   Rationale: the marker identifies one deployment (= one `cloud.yaml`), and two deployments at different paths get distinct markers even if they share a `compose_project` name. This also means Railway gets durable markers despite its ephemeral deploy dir.
3. **Refactor** `run_cloud_cli` so `_materialized_deploy_dir` is provider-aware. `destroy` clears the persistent deploy dir (after down succeeds); `_marker_dir` contents survive `destroy` by default so `cloud status --chain` works even after `destroy` — only `cloud init --force` or explicit cleanup resets it.
4. **Test** (`tests/test_cloud_persistent_dir.py`): build → exec → down on a `provider: local` spec reuses the same materialized dir; `_marker_dir` is stable across provider switches for a given `cloud.yaml` path.

### Step 8: Generalize session/attach glue (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py`) — unchanged from Rev 2/3
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `Provider.supports_session: bool` class attribute. Railway `True`; Local/SSH inherit `False`.
2. **Enforce** in `run_cloud_cli`: `getattr(args, "session", None)` + `not provider.supports_session` → `CliError("invalid_args", "--session is only supported for provider: railway")`.
3. **Test** (`tests/test_cloud_cli_session.py`).

### Step 9: Unlock `local` and `ssh` providers in the spec loader (`megaplan/cloud/spec.py`) — unchanged
**Scope:** Small
1. **Accept** `"railway" | "local" | "ssh"` in `load_spec`. `FUTURE_PROVIDERS = ("fly",)` with a distinct error.
2. **Add** `LocalSpec(compose_project, workdir)` and `SshSpec(host, user, port, identity_file, remote_dir, container)` dataclasses.
3. **Add** `local` / `ssh` optional fields to `CloudSpec`.
4. **Update tests:** drop reject-sprint-2 test; add happy paths + Fly future-provider error test.

### Step 10: `Provider` base + registry + file-transfer hooks (`megaplan/cloud/providers/base.py`, `providers/railway.py`) — unchanged
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `Provider.upload_file(src, dest)` and `Provider.read_remote_file(path)` hooks — base raises `CliError("not_implemented", ...)`; every provider overrides.
2. **Replace** `providers/base.py:52-54` hardcoded guard with a lazy `{"railway": ..., "local": ..., "ssh": ...}` factory.
3. **Implement** on `RailwayProvider`: `railway ssh -- "base64 -d > dest"` (stdin base64) for upload; `railway ssh -- cat <path>` for read.

### Step 11: Local provider (`megaplan/cloud/providers/local.py`, `megaplan/cloud/templates/docker-compose.yaml.tmpl`) — unchanged
**Scope:** Medium
1. Single-container `agent` service in `docker-compose.yaml.tmpl`, bind-mounted `./workspace` inside the persistent deploy dir.
2. `LocalProvider` implements every ABC method with `docker compose -p <proj> -f <deploy>/docker-compose.yaml ...`.
3. Missing `docker` → `CliError("provider_unavailable", ...)` with install link.
4. `template.py` materializes `docker-compose.yaml` when `spec.provider == "local"`.

### Step 12: SSH provider (`megaplan/cloud/providers/ssh.py`) — unchanged
**Scope:** Medium
1. `SshProvider` with `_remote_run` and `shlex.quote` on every interpolated value.
2. `build` rsyncs the persistent deploy dir, `docker build` remotely; `scp -r` fallback warns.
3. `deploy` writes `.env` remotely, `docker run -d --name <container> ...`.
4. All methods follow Railway's pattern over `_remote_run`.

### Step 13: Provider tests (`tests/test_cloud_local.py`, `tests/test_cloud_ssh.py`, `tests/test_cloud_local_lifecycle.py`) — unchanged
**Scope:** Medium
1. Mocked-subprocess unit tests with exact argv assertions.
2. `@pytest.mark.slow` lifecycle smoke: materialize → build → deploy → `ssh_exec("which megaplan")` → destroy.

### Step 14: Toolchain extensibility (`megaplan/cloud/spec.py`, `megaplan/cloud/template.py`, `megaplan/cloud/templates/Dockerfile`) — unchanged
**Scope:** Medium
1. `toolchains:` accepts string aliases (`rust`/`go`/`java`) or `{name, install}` mappings.
2. `TOOLCHAIN_BLOCK` placeholder in the Dockerfile; `_TOOLCHAIN_RECIPES` dict in `template.py`.
3. Commented example in `cloud.yaml.tmpl`.
4. Tests: alias recipe, custom snippet, unknown-alias rejection, byte-identical-baseline diff.

## Phase 3: Cloud wrappers + hardening + migration

### Step 15: `cloud chain` + `cloud bootstrap` with explicit path contract and marker persistence (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py`) — resolves FLAG-002, FLAG-006, correctness-2, issue_hints-1, issue_hints, callers
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** subparsers:
   - `chain` — positional `spec` (local path), optional `--idea-dir <local>` (default: spec's parent directory).
   - `bootstrap` — positional `idea_file` (local path), optional `--plan-name`, optional `--robustness` (default `standard`).
2. **`cloud chain` explicit path contract:**
   - Parse spec locally via `megaplan.chain.load_spec(spec_path)` — enumeration only.
   - Treat spec paths as **remote paths** (matches bundled `chain.yaml.example` + `docs/cloud.md`).
   - For each milestone: strip `spec.repo.workspace + "/"` prefix → relative tail. Resolve against `--idea-dir` (default spec's parent): `local_source = <idea_dir> / <tail>`. For absolute paths outside `workspace` or relative paths, resolve under `--idea-dir` directly.
   - Missing `local_source` → `CliError("missing_idea_file", "milestone 'X' idea not found on disk at <local_source>. Use --idea-dir to point at the directory containing ideas.")`.
   - `provider.upload_file(local_source, remote_path)` per file; upload the spec to `<workspace>/chain.yaml`.
   - `provider.ssh_exec(f"cd {shlex.quote(spec.repo.workspace)} && megaplan chain start --spec {shlex.quote(remote_spec_path)}")`.
3. **Persist chain marker (REV 4 FIX for FLAG-006, callers, issue_hints):** after successful upload + dispatch, write the marker to `_marker_dir(cloud_yaml_path) / "last_chain.json"` (not the deploy dir). Payload:
   ```json
   { "remote_spec": "<remote_spec_path>", "started_at": "<utc_iso>" }
   ```
   Because `_marker_dir` lives at `~/.megaplan/cloud/markers/<sha>/` regardless of provider, Railway's `TemporaryDirectory`-based deploy flow doesn't lose markers between `cloud chain` and `cloud status --chain` calls.
4. **`cloud bootstrap`:**
   - Upload idea file to `{workspace}/idea.txt`.
   - Build the remote command, passing `--name` only when `args.plan_name` is set:
     ```python
     cmd = f"cd {wx} && megaplan init --project-dir {wx} --idea-file {ideaq} --auto-start --robustness {rq}"
     if args.plan_name:
         cmd += f" --name {shlex.quote(args.plan_name)}"
     ```
   - `provider.ssh_exec(cmd)`; relay output.
5. **Cloud-side isolation preserved:** `chain.load_spec` is enumeration only; no chain-state parsing in cloud.

### Step 16: `cloud status --chain` with marker → fallback → override (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py`) — resolves FLAG-006, issue_hints-1, callers
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `--chain` + `--remote-spec <path>` flags to the existing `cloud status` subparser.
2. **Resolve the remote spec path** in this precedence order:
   1. Explicit `--remote-spec` argument.
   2. `_marker_dir(cloud_yaml_path) / "last_chain.json"` → `remote_spec` field (updated in Rev 4 — reads from the same provider-independent marker location Step 15 writes).
   3. `spec.chain.spec` from `cloud.yaml` when `spec.mode == "chain"`.
   4. Otherwise, `CliError("missing_remote_spec", "Unable to locate remote chain spec. Pass --remote-spec <path>, run `cloud chain <spec>` first, or set mode: chain in cloud.yaml.")`.
3. **Implementation:** `provider.read_remote_file(chain._state_path_for(Path(remote_spec)))` → parse with `ChainState.from_dict(json.loads(...))`. `provider.read_remote_file(remote_spec)` → write tempfile, `chain.load_spec(...)`. Call `chain.format_chain_status(spec, state)`. Emit structured + pretty payload.
4. **Contract test** (`tests/test_cloud_chain_status.py`): mock `provider.read_remote_file` + synthetic marker file; assert all three branches resolve correctly and the rendered payload is structurally identical to local `chain status --spec <same inputs>`.

### Step 17: Secret redaction incl. follow-mode streaming (`megaplan/cloud/redact.py`, `megaplan/cloud/providers/*`) — unchanged
**Scope:** Medium
1. `redact(text, secret_names, env=None)` — literal values (len ≥ 8), `NAME=value`/`NAME: value` patterns, `sk-.../ghp_.../xoxb-...` regexes → `***REDACTED***`.
2. `stream_redact(proc, secret_names, env=None)` — line-buffered Popen-iterator wrapper; flushes short-read tails on EOF.
3. Wire at `_relay_output`, `logs(follow=False)`, `logs(follow=True)` (Popen + `stream_redact`). `attach` is a documented gap.
4. `_logs_follow(argv)` helper factored across Railway/Local/SSH.
5. Tests: `tests/test_cloud_redact.py` + `tests/test_cloud_logs_follow_redacted.py`.

### Step 18: Missing-CLI errors with install links (`megaplan/cloud/providers/{base,railway,local,ssh}.py`) — unchanged
**Scope:** Small
1. `_missing_cli_error(binary, install_url)` helper in `base.py`.
2. Wire in constructors: Railway → railway docs URL; Local → docker docs URL; SSH → OpenSSH hint.
3. `tests/test_cloud_provider_unavailable.py`.

### Step 19: Retire `~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/` + update `docs/cloud.md` + `README` — unchanged
**Scope:** Small
1. **Draft** `docs/cloud-migration-from-reigh.md` with safe cleanup ordering:
   ```bash
   # Write the pointer FIRST, then clear the folder contents.
   cat > ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/MIGRATED.md <<'EOF'
   This folder has been migrated into the built-in `megaplan cloud` subcommand.
   See docs/cloud-migration-from-reigh.md in the megaplan repo for the runbook.
   EOF
   find ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev -mindepth 1 ! -name MIGRATED.md -print0 \
     | xargs -0 rm -rf
   ```
2. **Update** `docs/cloud.md`: sections for `provider: local` and `provider: ssh`; `cloud chain <spec>` workflow with `--idea-dir`; `cloud bootstrap` no-`--plan-name` default; `cloud status --chain` precedence (`--remote-spec` > marker > `spec.chain.spec`); `toolchains:` block; follow-mode redaction + attach gap. **Note the marker lives at `~/.megaplan/cloud/markers/<sha>/last_chain.json`** — documented so users who inspect state on disk understand where to look.
3. **Update** `README.md` "Cloud runs" section briefly; preserves the `README.md:108-113` pointer to `docs/cloud.md`.
4. **Update** `cloud.yaml.tmpl` with commented `toolchains:` + local/ssh examples.

---

## Execution Order
1. **Phase 1 (Steps 1–6).** Core commands + parser isolation + launcher fix. Step 4's argv normalization + lazy cloud subparser land before Step 6's isolation + `cloud --help` + `cli_entry` tests.
2. **Phase 2 foundations (Steps 7, 8, 9, 10)** before concrete providers (11, 12).
3. **Concrete providers (Steps 11, 12) + tests (13)** parallelizable.
4. **Toolchain (Step 14)** independent.
5. **Phase 3 Step 15** before Step 16 (status reads the marker). Step 17 after providers exist.
6. **Step 19** last.

## Validation Order
1. Core unit tests first: `pytest tests/test_chain.py tests/test_handle_init_idea_file.py tests/test_core_without_cloud.py tests/test_cli_entry.py -x`. Proves core/cloud isolation AND argv-normalization path.
2. Launcher-path regression: `pytest tests/test_auto.py tests/test_chain.py -x`.
3. Cloud unit tests: `pytest tests/test_cloud_spec.py tests/test_cloud_persistent_dir.py tests/test_cloud_cli_session.py tests/test_cloud_local.py tests/test_cloud_ssh.py tests/test_cloud_template.py tests/test_cloud_redact.py tests/test_cloud_logs_follow_redacted.py tests/test_cloud_provider_unavailable.py tests/test_cloud_chain_status.py -x`.
4. Chain wrapper integration: `pytest tests/test_cloud_chain_wrapper.py -x` — path contract + marker persistence at `~/.megaplan/cloud/markers/<sha>/last_chain.json`.
5. Full suite — no regression; v0.19.0 `cloud.yaml` surface intact.
6. `@pytest.mark.slow` smoke on docker host.
7. Manual end-to-end: `provider: local` + `cloud bootstrap` + `cloud status --chain` via marker + `cloud destroy`.
8. Reigh migration — human-run per Step 19.

  "assumptions": [
    "`megaplan chain start --spec PATH` is a named subcommand under the existing `chain` parser and drives through the same `run_chain(...)` path; bare `megaplan chain --spec` stays as a backcompat alias.",
    "`format_chain_status(spec, state)` lives in `megaplan.chain` (core) and is reused verbatim by `cloud status --chain` (SD-001).",
    "`--auto-start` on `megaplan init` runs `megaplan.auto.drive()` in-process and blocks until terminal (SD-005).",
    "`toolchains:` accepts a mixed list: string aliases (`rust`/`go`/`java`) or `{name, install}` mappings. Empty list preserves the v0.19.0 Dockerfile byte-for-byte (SD-004).",
    "Local provider uses single-container `docker compose` with bind-mounted `./workspace` inside a persistent per-project deploy dir under `~/.megaplan/cloud/<compose_project>/` (SD-006); Railway keeps `TemporaryDirectory`.",
    "SSH provider uses plain `ssh`+`docker` over a bash login shell; rsync with `scp -r` fallback; single named container per host.",
    "Fly provider skipped; `provider: fly` surfaces a dedicated future-provider error (SD-002).",
    "Secret redaction applies on every emit path including default follow-mode `logs -f` via a line-buffered `stream_redact` Popen wrapper (SD-007); `attach` remains a documented gap.",
    "Lazy cloud subparser mechanism: `build_cloud_parser` is split into `_register_cloud_subcommands(parser)` + thin top-level wrapper (SD-009). `main()` normalizes `argv = sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else argv` before the lazy intercept keys on `argv[0] == 'cloud'`, so `cli_entry()` invocations work.",
    "`cloud chain` treats spec paths as remote; local sources are discovered by stripping `spec.repo.workspace` prefix and looking under `--idea-dir` (default: spec's parent) (SD-011).",
    "`cloud chain` writes `last_chain.json` to a provider-independent `_marker_dir(cloud_yaml_path) = ~/.megaplan/cloud/markers/<sha256(abs_path)[:16]>/`; `cloud status --chain` reads from the same location. Precedence is `--remote-spec` > marker > `spec.chain.spec` (SD-012, Rev 4 updates the path).",
    "`cloud bootstrap --plan-name` is optional; omitted invocations delegate to core's slug-based default.",
    "Local subprocess launchers in `auto.py` / `chain.py` use `sys.executable -m megaplan` (SD-010).",
    "Reigh retirement runbook writes `MIGRATED.md` before clearing siblings via `find -mindepth 1 ! -name MIGRATED.md -delete` (SD-013); actual shell commands are human-executed.",
    "Documentation scope explicitly includes `docs/cloud.md` (SD-014) alongside `README.md` and `cloud.yaml.tmpl`; Rev 4 adds a mention of the new marker location under `~/.megaplan/cloud/markers/<sha>/`.",
    "No version bump or CHANGELOG in this plan \u2014 sprint 2 ships as v0.20.0 via a separate follow-up commit (SD-003)."
  ],

    {
      "id": "DEBT-014",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: i checked the skip caller path against the real hook behavior and `removefromqueue(entryid)` looks redundant. `scoring.skipentry(entryid)` optimistically adds the id to `skippedentryids` in `src/hooks/usescoring.ts:245-249`, and `usecarouselqueue` already reactively filters skipped entries from `submissions[]` in `src/hooks/usecarouselqueue.ts:154-165`. calling `removefromqueue()` immediately afterward is safe, but it duplicates an existing state transition and should be justified as an explicit ux optimization rather than required logic.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "move-the-entry-page-into-20260401-0450"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-015",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the direct ui sign-in caller is still important here. `scorepanel` invokes its own `onrequiresignin` prop when an unauthenticated user interacts with the score ui (`src/components/scorepanel.tsx:499-515`), which is independent of the scoring hook's internal `requireauth()` path. the plan should be read as requiring that caller to dispatch `open_sign_in_modal`; otherwise one caller remains broken even if `submitscore()` is correctly wired.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "move-the-entry-page-into-20260401-0450"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-034",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: i rechecked the caller paths after the revision. the raw-schema caller path through `validate_payload()` is now accounted for, but the runtime-schema caller path still is not: `strict_schema()` only reaches the on-disk runtime schemas through `ensure_runtime_layout()`, while current tests like `tests/test_schemas.py` and `tests/test_parallel_review.py` consume the already-written repo copy directly. the plan does not yet say how that caller path gets refreshed or asserted after the code change.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [

    },

      "id": "DEBT-051",

- Every check must end with at least one finding. Never leave a `findings` array empty. If you found no issue, add one detailed `flagged: false` finding explaining what you checked and why it appears clear.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}
Bad: {"detail": "No issue found", "flagged": false}  ← too brief, will be rejected
Bad: {"detail": "The hints suggest approach X but the plan uses Y. However Y is consistent with X's intent.", "flagged": false}  ← a different approach than the hints IS a flag. You found a divergence — flag it. The gate decides if it's acceptable.
